# ANGULAR-OBJECTIVE-01 preregistered comparison

ANGULAR_EXECUTION_STATE: COMPLETE

Preregistration SHA256: `d75741b7e072fe73f23a7c71f894b67deeedf46a0263b6d19dca7d4d2a1ae12e`.

No numerical calibration fit, physical-scene camera metric, study arm, or evidence artifact has run. The package is frozen for independent root/Astra review; it does not authorize execution or production integration. The proposed evidence path `docs/reports/ANGULAR-OBJECTIVE-01-evidence-2026-10-05.jsonl.gz` is absent. The 12 GUI-BORDER and CAM-MD families are consumed forensic data and are forbidden for this study's inference, evaluation, tuning, or threshold selection.

Both arms use the accepted GUI-border treatment, including the exact single delegated automatic mask, detector output/order and whole-segment filter. A delegates the original pixel/length `_score` unchanged. B changes that delegate only:

`S_ang = sum_i L_i max(1 - theta_i / tau, 0)`

`theta_i = asin(clip(2 r_i / L_i, 0, 1))`, `tau = asin(6/120) = 0.050020856805770016 radians = 2.865983982599 degrees`.

Here `r_i` is the minimum accepted endpoint-displacement residual over the three Manhattan directions; `L_i` is the original native Euclidean segment length, not a rescaled confidence weight. Tau is a fixed theory normalization using the current 3-pixel threshold and 120-pixel reference length. No threshold sweep, new normalization, objective selection, or other hyperparameter is permitted. Zero-length lines contribute zero. Clipping preserves the formula under residual/length roundoff. Finite, infinite and singular midpoint vanishing points retain the accepted geometry helper's epsilon convention; the experiment adds no special-direction veto.

RGB entry, GUI mask, enhancement, detector, retained lines, lengths, ordering, >=12 support, longest-300 coarse subset, fixed coarse/fine candidate grid, refinement algorithm and >=2% acceptance margin remain unchanged and source-pinned. Existing CalibrationResult inlier/residual fields keep their **pixel threshold-3** meaning in both arms. Separate angular support and per-axis diagnostics are evaluator outputs, with no axis or confidence gate. If the fixed filter leaves <12 retained lines, the existing support refusal occurs before the objective; that case is outside this loss intervention. The previous three support refusals cannot be recovered by changing this objective while the support policy stays fixed.

The new registry contains 12 ray-rendered physical families, four planar, four stepped and four occluding. Every family has one native crop and one GUI-only contamination variant: 36 registered pairs, 72 future arms. Four fresh yaw/pitch/FOV/frame settings include positive and negative pitches. PCG64 seeds, eye/layout/nearest-hit geometry, native16 texture seeds and seam widths are bound below. All 36 RGB hashes are distinct and disjoint from both consumed registries. Camera truth and full world geometry hashes are separately bound. Crops preserve parent image bytes and shift the principal point; GUI-only changes are confined to the automatic excluded region. These stimuli were only rendered for preregistration and no-fit QA. No rendered-camera accuracy result exists.

Each arm runs the same accepted call-through AST/caller-code trace. A leaves the original scorer installed. B temporarily supplies an owner-thread checked angular delegate before the accepted tracer captures its delegated original. The unchanged optimizer calls it exactly as it would the pixel scorer. Objective/tracer/filter locks reject nested or concurrent installation and original function identities restore in finally, including exceptions and interruption. The package binds whole sources, trace AST spans and runtime versions; drift refuses before inference. Input support and the full coarse camera argument enumeration must agree between arms before a pair can seal. Objective-dependent fine/refinement winners may differ.

All 36 complete A/B predictions, support snapshots and traces must be sealed and durably retained before **any** evaluator truth access, physical metric, renderer cross-score or nearest-truth-grid rank. Full sealed payload plus SHA is written as one independent stdlib gzip JSONL member per pair, after an experiment/preregistration-bound header. The report is exclusively locked and fsynced from NOT_STARTED to RUNNING before stimulus rendering/inference. Evidence is exclusively created; an existing file or RUNNING/COMPLETE marker refuses a retry. No repair, truncation, recreation or rerun is allowed after a failure. Earlier complete members remain if a later scene fails; a partial final member invalidates replay and blocks evaluation. Only descriptors/seals, not all full traces, accumulate in memory.

Evidence limits include gzip headers/trailers: 512 MiB compressed total, 16 MiB compressed per member, 200,000,000 bytes decompressed per member; trace/filter caps are bound below. Each finalized member is flushed/fsynced and read back immediately. One bounded full-stream replay, including canonical row/order/seal/CRC/trailer/digest checks, must finish before the first evaluator. Evaluation then reads members by verified descriptor; the final compressed digest must match replay before COMPLETE/result append. Missing, malformed, truncated, extra, reordered or mutated evidence refuses. No actual `.jsonl.gz` file has been created by QA; lifecycle tests use memory objects and evaluator stubs.

Post-seal diagnostics use the **same full retained lines and native lengths** to cross-score every emitted and renderer camera under both losses. They report normalized score/total-length, per-axis support and both residual representations, with pixel-labeled result fields kept separate. Actual native coarse scores are labeled as longest-300-subset scores and never directly compared to full-set scores. Nearest renderer-camera coarse-grid coordinates are selected only after seal, using focal absolute distance, yaw modulo90 distance and pitch absolute distance. Ties retain the highest actual native coarse score; competition rank is 1 plus the number of actual candidates scoring strictly higher. This is a discrete diagnostic, not truth-seeded inference or continuous coverage. Each nearest/winner candidate is additionally cross-scored on the full retained set. Captured coarse winner-to-full-rescore, fine winner-to-refinement, refinement acceptance and final prediction identify actual transitions; per-tag `candidate_max` means highest tested candidate and makes no optimizer-retention claim. Trace overhead and score-delegate runtime are reported separately.

Diagnostic camera gates remain focal error <=5%, orientation error modulo90 <=1 degree, principal error <=1% frame diagonal. The pilot requires B >=9/12 primary passes, >=3 net passes over A, at least three A-wrong/B-pass corrected families, zero lost A-pass families, zero added wrong emissions in the 24 correlated crop/GUI stress cases, and GUI-only prediction stability within each arm. Refusal accounting is not accuracy recovery. The stress variants are correlated and do not inflate the 12-family primary denominator. No M3/product support or confidence gate changes. A pilot pass permits a bounded integration review and independent real screenshots, not automatic integration. If an incorrect camera remains preferred, the fixed loss is rejected; a coarse-grid/search miss motivates a separately preregistered search study; support failures motivate better evidence without support relaxation. These are future interpretations, not measured results.

No-fit command in the pinned .venv CPU environment (project workdir, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1, PYTHONPATH=src):

```powershell
.venv/Scripts/python.exe -B -m pytest tests/test_angular_objective.py -q -o addopts= -p no:cacheprovider
```

Result: **85 passed in 6.24s, exit 0**. Initial setup run had 77 passes and two test-only errors (mask height/width API and decoded-cap key); corrected without recipe, filter, score, solver or gate change. The suite covers scorer algebra/length normalization/tau/clipping, finite/infinite/singular geometry, invalid arrays/weights/threshold, fresh deterministic registration/no collision, crop/GUI stimulus behavior, accepted upstream flow with detector/camera stubs, same filter/order/support and unchanged bytes/result labels, A-original/B-angular delegation, owner/restoration/refusal, coarse rank and selected transitions with synthetic records, every source/runtime drift, seal tamper, memory-only gzip caps/CRC/trailer/order/digest/prefix/ownership/short-write/one-shot and all-seals evaluator barrier, final digest veto and COMPLETE append. No physical optimizer parity claim is made by these stub tests; accepted CAM's independent parity suite remains source-bound. Pure analytic line scores are not camera fits or physical-scene camera metrics. AST parsing, byte trailing whitespace/tab/newline checks, and owned git diff whitespace verification are checked before final handoff.

Until then preserve NOT_STARTED and absent evidence. No accuracy, objective recovery, generalization or product integration claim.
